Cell 58: # Summary bar chart comparing all metrics model_names = list...
============================================================

================================================================================
Final Summary: Model Comparison on H2O - Standard vs Self-Attention
================================================================================

Lower values indicate closer agreement with PBE reference.

Key observations:
- Pre-trained models should reproduce PBE well (fitted to PBE enhancement factors)
- Energy-only training optimizes for total energy, may drift from PBE density
- DM regularization adds penalty on density matrix, may constrain density changes
- Grid density training explicitly minimizes density error on the grid
- Self-attention networks may capture additional feature interactions

Compare (Attn) variants with their standard counterparts to assess self-attention impact.
